The critique is complete and written to `/home/mmoniz/trading-journal/reports/deepseek_mechanism_prereg_2026-10-26.md`. Read-only throughout: I read the prereg and all context files, plus ran only non-mutating greps to verify key claims; no code was executed, no DB run, no source file touched.

## What I verified (vs. what's UNVERIFIED)

**Verified against real files:**
- **Date split is genuinely out-of-window** — `calibrate_live_roster.mjs` selects `trade_date >= '2026-07-09'`; Test 0 uses strictly `< 2026-07-09`.
- **The "5-bar approach" direction rule matches live** — `acd.js:3742` (`last5[0].close < currentPrice ? FROM_BELOW : FROM_ABOVE`) is algebraically identical to the replay's `long = !(C[gi-5] < C[gi])`. The direction rule is *not* the fairness problem; the exit/gates are.
- **`ret60` horizon truncation** — the builder nulls `ret60` when `gi+60 > b−1`, so every touch after **15:00** is silently dropped from outcome tests.
- **In-sample level universe** — `update_optimal_stops.mjs` writes `OPTIMAL_STOP` with `window_days=9999`, and the builder filters levels by any such row with no run_date bound.

**Marked UNVERIFIED** (DB-dependent): all sample sizes / SDs (so the power estimate is a labelled formula), the "+14/247/3 passed" figures, whether live proximity uses close vs high/low, and `level_prices` causality for same-day levels.

## Top findings (ranked)

- **S1 (Critical):** the `e` axis is degenerate — new-extreme touches have `e ∈ {0,1}` by construction, so "top-tercile `e`" just re-sorts the survivors' own "fade a fresh extreme" shape; a SHOWN `E` would be near-tautological, not a mechanism.
- **S2 (Critical):** Test 0's NOT REPLICATED → "no out-of-window support" is too strong, because exit-free `ret60` tests only the gate-free entry signal, not the live `OPTIMAL_STOP`-exit + gate-stack trade.
- **S3 (High):** post-15:00 touches dropped from the outcome = selection on the outcome variable.
- **S4 (High):** the control (inherited `f`, not near a level/extreme) conflates "the level" with "an intraday extreme in the fade direction."
- **S5–S10:** `1/nLevels` mis-applied at cell/family level; power ≈ the +14 claim (marginal); in-sample universe; 20 tests as two separate BH families; `b`-axis confounds; coarse control bucket.

The report ends with **9 verbatim-adoptable amendments** (prior-bar `e`, level/excess-conditioned control, per-cell weight 1, held-to-close fallback for late touches, stated power/universe/multiplicity, era/robustness splits) and a bottom-line on what a null vs. replicate would actually mean.
